### [HIGH] — Silent exception swallow (except: pass)

### [MEDIUM] — asyncio.sleep(0) used as ordering yield

**File:** `agents\streaming_agent.py:181`  
**Layer:** backend

```
await asyncio.sleep(0)  # Yield control to event loop
```

**Why it matters:** Yields control to let other tasks run. Removing it (or adding any await before it) may break assumed ordering — heisenbug behaviour.

**Fix:** Use an explicit asyncio.Event or Queue to coordinate instead.

## [Schrodinbug] Schrodinbug

> Works fine until you read the code and realise it shouldn't

**572 finding(s)**

### [MEDIUM] — Direct dict key access on external data

**File:** `agents\prediction_arbitrage_analyst.py:115`  
**Layer:** backend

```
log_data["error_type"] = run.error_type
```

**Why it matters:** KeyError if key is absent — works until it is.

**Fix:** Use `.get('key', default)` or validate with Pydantic.

### [MEDIUM] — Direct dict key access on external data

**File:** `agents\prediction_arbitrage_analyst.py:117`  
**Layer:** backend

```
log_data["error_message"] = run.error_message
```

**Why it matters:** KeyError if key is absent — works until it is.

**Fix:** Use `.get('key', default)` or validate with Pydantic.

### [MEDIUM] — Direct dict key access on external data

**File:** `agents\reflection\core.py:90`  
**Layer:** backend

```
data['outcome'] = self.outcome.value
```

**Why it matters:** KeyError if key is absent — works until it is.

**Fix:** Use `.get('key', default)` or validate with Pydantic.

### [MEDIUM] — Direct dict key access on external data

**File:** `agents\reflection\core.py:91`  
**Layer:** backend

```
data['pattern_tags'] = list(self.pattern_tags)
```

**Why it matters:** KeyError if key is absent — works until it is.

**Fix:** Use `.get('key', default)` or validate with Pydantic.

### [MEDIUM] — Direct dict key access on external data

**File:** `agents\reflection\core.py:98`  
**Layer:** backend

```
data['outcome'] = DecisionOutcome(data['outcome'])
```

## [Race] Race Condition

> Two async operations fighting over shared state

**279 finding(s)**

### [HIGH] — asyncio.gather with shared mutable container

**File:** `merid\agents\base.py:367`  
**Layer:** backend

```
results = await asyncio.gather(
```

**Why it matters:** Coroutines launched via gather() all write to the same list/dict with no Lock. In CPython the GIL doesn't protect asyncio tasks.

**Fix:** Accumulate results from gather() return values, not a shared container.

### [HIGH] — asyncio.gather with shared mutable container

**File:** `merid\loop.py:515`  
**Layer:** backend

```
await asyncio.gather(*parallel_coros)
```

**Why it matters:** Coroutines launched via gather() all write to the same list/dict with no Lock. In CPython the GIL doesn't protect asyncio tasks.

**Fix:** Accumulate results from gather() return values, not a shared container.

### [HIGH] — asyncio.gather with shared mutable container

**File:** `web\api\real_data_endpoints.py:837`  
**Layer:** backend

```
probes = await asyncio.gather(*tasks, return_exceptions=True)
```

**Why it matters:** Coroutines launched via gather() all write to the same list/dict with no Lock. In CPython the GIL doesn't protect asyncio tasks.

**Fix:** Accumulate results from gather() return values, not a shared container.

### [HIGH] — Async fetch in useEffect without AbortController

**File:** `web\react\src\components\KalshiActivityLogEnhanced.tsx:375`  
**Layer:** frontend

```
useEffect(() => {
```

**Why it matters:** If the component unmounts or deps change before the fetch resolves, the callback still fires and calls setState on an unmounted component — classic race condition.

**Fix:** Add AbortController; return () => controller.abort() in cleanup.

### [MEDIUM] — Dual data sources: WebSocket + polling for same component

**File:** `web\react\src\views\KalshiPortfolioView.tsx:45`  
**Layer:** frontend

```
const pollOpts = { pollingInterval: DEFAULTS.POLLING_INTERVALS.STANDARD };
```

**Why it matters:** Component uses both a WS stream and HTTP polling. Both can update state concurrently — last writer wins, causing flicker or stale overwrite.

**Fix:** Gate polling behind `!wsConnected`; one source of truth per data domain.

## [MemLeak] Memory Leak

> Unreleased subscriptions, timers, or event listeners

**65 finding(s)**

### [MEDIUM] — asyncio.create_task result not stored

**File:** `consensus\consensus_coordinator.py:667`  
**Layer:** backend

```
asyncio.create_task(self._handle_round_timeout(round.round_id, symbol))
```

**Why it matters:** Task reference is discarded. CPython may GC the task unexpectedly (Python 3.12 warning), and there is no handle to cancel it on shutdown.

**Fix:** Store in a set: `_tasks.add(t := asyncio.create_task(...)); t.add_done_callback(_tasks.discard)`

## [Regression] Regression

**3 finding(s)**

### [CRITICAL] — Shared abstraction modified: `config/trading_constants.py`

**File:** `config/trading_constants.py:1`  
**Layer:** cross-boundary

**Why it matters:** Financial magic numbers — affects all position sizing. Recent modification to this file is high-risk: all consumers could be affected.

### [CRITICAL] — Shared abstraction modified: `web/main.py`

**File:** `web/main.py:1`  
**Layer:** cross-boundary

**Why it matters:** FastAPI app — router registration, middleware, startup. Recent modification to this file is high-risk: all consumers could be affected.

```

```

**Why it matters:** Removing assertions weakens the test suite — regressions covered by those assertions will now pass CI silently.

## [PhaseOfMoon] Phase Of Moon

> Only reproduces under specific environmental conditions

**485 finding(s)**

### [HIGH] — datetime.now() without timezone (naive datetime)

**File:** `agents\crypto_prediction_agent.py:96`  
**Layer:** backend

```
"last_updated": datetime.now()
```

**Why it matters:** Naive datetimes are ambiguous during DST transitions. Comparisons with timezone-aware datetimes raise TypeError at runtime — only during DST changeover hours.

**Fix:** Use `datetime.now(timezone.utc)` or `datetime.utcnow().replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)`

### [HIGH] — datetime.now() without timezone (naive datetime)

**File:** `agents\crypto_prediction_agent.py:190`  
**Layer:** backend

```

```

**Why it matters:** Naive datetimes are ambiguous during DST transitions. Comparisons with timezone-aware datetimes raise TypeError at runtime — only during DST changeover hours.

**Fix:** Use `datetime.now(timezone.utc)` or `datetime.utcnow().replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)`

### [MEDIUM] — os.getenv() inside request handler

**File:** `web\api\system_endpoints.py:647`  
**Layer:** backend

```
version = os.getenv("MERID_VERSION", "")
```

**Why it matters:** Env vars read at request time can change between deployments without a restart — behaviour differs depending on when the request arrives.

**Fix:** Read env vars once at module/startup level and inject via dependency.

### [MEDIUM] — os.getenv() inside request handler

```

```

**Why it matters:** Env vars read at request time can change between deployments without a restart — behaviour differs depending on when the request arrives.

**Fix:** Read env vars once at module/startup level and inject via dependency.

### [MEDIUM] — Locale-sensitive date format without explicit locale

**File:** `web\react\src\components\AgentLeaderboard.tsx:206`  
**Layer:** frontend

```
<span className="text-xs text-slate-500">{new Date(entry.timestamp).toLocaleTimeString()}</span>
```

**Why it matters:** Output depends on the user's OS locale. A user in a non-US locale may see different date ordering — bugs only surface on specific machines.

**Fix:** Pass explicit locale: `.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { ... })`
